Compare sedimentation before and after flocculation

CL-0070 · VisionariesAI Labs Private Limited · Virtual practical

Aim

Compare matched dilute suspensions containing unflocculated primary particles and prepared ideal porous aggregates. Observe settling fronts, clear-water depth and growing wet sediment while conserving the original dry solid and water.

Principle and apparatus

Matched vertical settling jars, chosen mineral-density reference2650kg/m³, water, prepared aggregate-size references, timer, height scale and magnified structural/bed views. Chosen Df2 aggregates use Rf=r√N and include internal water. Low-Re equivalent-sphere drag and dilute batch counterflow set a reference front speed. The wet bed has chosen object packing0.6; it contains pore water. This comparison does not predict coagulant dose, chemical aggregation kinetics or measured real-floc permeability.

Procedure

Prepare matched water and dry-solid amounts, primary radius and viscosity. Choose the already prepared aggregate size; begin both uniformly suspended at the same time. Record both initial states. Let the samples settle undisturbed; pause at matched physical times and measure cloud top, clear depth and wet-bed height. Record settled/suspended dry mass and water in bed, cloud and clear layer. Repeat the no-aggregation control and changes to radius, viscosity or prepared size. Distinguish representative enlarged markers from actual material quantity. Use the worked substitutions with units, check water/solid/mass conservation, then write result, conclusion and limitations.

Prediction

Prepared flocs should clear faster while retaining water and dry solid. <angle> & reasoning.

Formula substitutions and worked results

Trial 1: Unflocculated primary particles

  1. Primary radius conversion

    r = rµm ×10⁻⁶

    Substitution: 2.000000 ×10⁻⁶

    Result: 2.00000000e-6 m

  2. Primaries per settling object

    N = 1 for primary reference

    Substitution: Unflocculated single primary

    Result: 1.000000 primaries/object

  3. Chosen effective drag radius

    Rf = r√N

    Substitution: 2.00000000e-6 ×√1

    Result: 2.00000000e-6 m; chosen Df2 reference

  4. One primary solid volume

    Vp = 4πr³/3

    Substitution: 4π × 2.00000000e-6³/3

    Result: 3.35103216e-17 m³

  5. Effective object envelope

    Vf = 4πRf³/3

    Substitution: 4π × 2.00000000e-6³/3

    Result: 3.35103216e-17 m³; includes internal water

  6. Internal aggregate solid fraction

    φf = N Vp/Vf

    Substitution: 1 × 3.35103216e-17/3.35103216e-17

    Result: 1.000000 fraction

  7. Effective wet-object density

    ρf = ρw+(ρs−ρw)φf

    Substitution: 1000+(2650−1000) × 1.000000

    Result: 2650.000000 kg/m³

  8. Dynamic viscosity conversion

    µ = µmPa·s/1000

    Substitution: 1.000000/1000

    Result: 0.001 Pa·s

  9. Dry solid per settling object

    mobject = ρs N Vp

    Substitution: 2650 × 1 × 3.35103216e-17

    Result: 8.88023523e-14 kg

  10. Physical object-count reference

    Nobjects = (m₀ ×10⁻⁶)/mobject

    Substitution: (50.000000 ×10⁻⁶)/8.88023523e-14

    Result: 563048147.730762 objects; continuum average, not displayed marker count

  11. Original solid volume

    Vs = (m₀ ×10⁻⁶/ρs) ×10⁶

    Substitution: (50.000000 ×10⁻⁶/2650) ×10⁶

    Result: 0.01886792453 mL

  12. Initial mixture volume

    V₀ = Vwater+Vs

    Substitution: 100.000000+0.01886792453

    Result: 100.018868 mL

  13. Initial solid volume fraction

    φ₀ = Vs/V₀

    Substitution: 0.01886792453/100.018868

    Result: 1.88643652e-4 fraction

  14. Dilute object-envelope fraction

    φobjects = φ₀/φf

    Substitution: 1.88643652e-4/1.000000

    Result: 1.88643652e-4 fraction; below0.005 in allowed preparations

  15. Fluid-relative terminal reference

    vrel = 2(ρf−ρw)gRf²/(9µ)

    Substitution: 2 × (2650.000000−1000) × 9.81 × 2.00000000e-6²/(9 × 0.001)

    Result: 1.43880000e-5 m/s; equivalent-sphere low-Re reference

  16. Closed-batch front speed

    v = (1−φobjects)vrel

    Substitution: (1−1.88643652e-4) × 1.43880000e-5

    Result: 1.43852858e-5 m/s; dilute zero-volume-flux correction

  17. External carrier counterflow reference

    uw = φobjects vrel

    Substitution: 1.88643652e-4 × 1.43880000e-5

    Result: 2.71420487e-9 m/s upward during the uniform settling zone

  18. Particle Reynolds number

    Re = 2Rf ρw vrel/µ

    Substitution: 2 × 2.00000000e-6 × 1000 × 1.43880000e-5/0.001

    Result: 5.75520000e-5 dimensionless; below0.04 in allowed preparations

  19. Initial column height

    H = V₀/A

    Substitution: 100.018868/10

    Result: 10.001887 cm

  20. Wet-bed solid fraction

    β = packing ×φf

    Substitution: 0.6 × 1.000000

    Result: 0.6 fraction; chosen constant wet-bed packing

  21. Final wet-bed height

    Bmax = Vs/(Aβ)

    Substitution: 0.01886792453/(10 × 0.6)

    Result: 0.003144654088 cm

  22. Two-front settling completion

    tend = (H−Bmax)/(100v)

    Substitution: (10.001887−0.003144654088)/(100 × 1.43852858e-5)

    Result: 6950.673265 s; terminal-reference batch model

  23. Settled dry-mass fraction

    f = min(1,t/tend)

    Substitution: min(1,0/6950.673265)

    Result: 0 fraction

  24. Growing wet-bed height

    b = Bmax f

    Substitution: 0.003144654088 × 0

    Result: 0 cm

  25. Suspension upper front

    zcloud = max(b,H−100vt)

    Substitution: max(0,10.001887−100 × 1.43852858e-5 × 0)

    Result: 10.001887 cm above inside base

  26. Remaining suspension height

    hcloud = zcloud−b

    Substitution: 10.001887−0

    Result: 10.001887 cm

  27. Clear upper-water depth

    hclear = H−zcloud

    Substitution: 10.001887−10.001887

    Result: 0 cm

  28. Settled dry solid

    mbed = m₀f

    Substitution: 50.000000 × 0

    Result: 0 mg

  29. Suspended dry solid

    mcloud = m₀−mbed

    Substitution: 50.000000−0

    Result: 50.000000 mg

  30. Settled solid-only volume

    Vs,bed = Vs f

    Substitution: 0.01886792453 × 0

    Result: 0 mL

  31. Suspended solid-only volume

    Vs,cloud = Vs−Vs,bed

    Substitution: 0.01886792453−0

    Result: 0.01886792453 mL

Trial 2: Prepared porous-floc reference

  1. Primary radius conversion

    Substitution: 1.000000/1000

  9. Dry solid per settling object

    Substitution: 65.452838−0.0123472625

    Result: 65.440491 mL

  37. Retained water inventory

    Vw = Vw,bed+Vw,cloud+Vclear

    Substitution: 0.08042149838+65.440491+34.479087

    Result: 100.000000 mL; equals original water

  38. Local cloud concentration

    ccloud = mcloud/(Vcloud/1000)

    Substitution: 32.720246/(65.452838/1000)

    Result: 499.905678 mg/L; local cloud stays uniform until it disappears

  39. Effective gravity minus buoyancy

    Feff = (ρf−ρw)Vf g

    Substitution: (1206.250000−1000) × 1.71572847e-14 × 9.81

    Result: 3.47145486e-11 N

  40. Viscous terminal drag

    Fdrag = 6πµRf vrel

    Substitution: 6π × 0.001 × 1.60000000e-5 × 1.15104000e-4

    Result: 3.47145486e-11 N; equals effective gravity

  41. Wet-bed mass

    Mbed = ρw Vw,bed/1000+mbed/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 0.08042149838/1000+17.279754/1000

    Result: 0.09770125277 g; mL to water mass, mg to g

  42. Cloud mass

    Mcloud = ρw Vw,cloud/1000+mcloud/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 65.440491/1000+32.720246/1000

    Result: 65.473211 g

  43. Clear-water mass

    Mclear = ρw Vclear/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 34.479087/1000

    Result: 34.479087 g

  44. Initial whole-sample mass

    M₀ = ρw Vwater/1000+m₀/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 100.000000/1000+50.000000/1000

    Result: 100.050000 g

  45. Whole-sample mass check

    M = Mbed+Mcloud+Mclear

    Substitution: 0.09770125277+65.473211+34.479087

    Result: 100.050000 g; equals initial mass

  46. Dry solid inventory check

    m = mbed+mcloud

    Substitution: 17.279754+32.720246

    Result: 50.000000 mg; equals original dry solid

  47. Terminal relaxation reference

    Substitution: 2 × 1206.250000 × 1.60000000e-5²/(9 × 0.001)

Trial 5: Unflocculated primary particles

  1. Primary radius conversion

    Result: 2.00000000e-6 m

  3. Chosen effective drag radius

    Substitution: 2.00000000e-6 ×√1

    Result: 2.00000000e-6 m; chosen Df2 reference

    Substitution: 4π × 2.00000000e-6³/3

    Result: 1.000000 fraction

  7. Effective wet-object density

    ρf = ρw+(ρs−ρw)φf

    Substitution: 1000+(2650−1000) × 1.000000

    Result: 2650.000000 kg/m³

  8. Dynamic viscosity conversion

    µ = µmPa·s/1000

    Substitution: 1.000000/1000

    Result: 0.001 Pa·s

  9. Dry solid per settling object

    mobject = ρs N Vp

    Substitution: 2650 × 1 × 3.35103216e-17

    Result: 8.88023523e-14 kg

    Substitution: (50.000000 ×10⁻⁶)/8.88023523e-14

    Substitution: 100.000000+0.01886792453

    Result: 100.018868 mL

    Substitution: 1.88643652e-4/1.000000

  16. Closed-batch front speed

    Substitution: (1−1.88643652e-4) × 1.43880000e-5

  17. External carrier counterflow reference

    Substitution: 1.88643652e-4 × 1.43880000e-5

Trial 20: Prepared porous-floc reference

  1. Primary radius conversion

    r = rµm ×10⁻⁶

    Substitution: 2.000000 ×10⁻⁶

    Result: 2.00000000e-6 m

  2. Primaries per settling object

    N = chosen prepared aggregate

    Substitution: 1.000000

    Result: 1.000000 primaries/object

  3. Chosen effective drag radius

    Rf = r√N

    Substitution: 2.00000000e-6 ×√1

    Result: 2.00000000e-6 m; chosen Df2 reference

  4. One primary solid volume

    Vp = 4πr³/3

    Substitution: 4π × 2.00000000e-6³/3

    Result: 3.35103216e-17 m³

  5. Effective object envelope

    Vf = 4πRf³/3

    Substitution: 4π × 2.00000000e-6³/3

    Result: 3.35103216e-17 m³; includes internal water

  6. Internal aggregate solid fraction

    φf = N Vp/Vf

    Substitution: 1 × 3.35103216e-17/3.35103216e-17

    Result: 1.000000 fraction

  7. Effective wet-object density

    ρf = ρw+(ρs−ρw)φf

    Substitution: 1000+(2650−1000) × 1.000000

    Result: 2650.000000 kg/m³

  8. Dynamic viscosity conversion

    µ = µmPa·s/1000

    Substitution: 1.000000/1000

    Result: 0.001 Pa·s

  9. Dry solid per settling object

    mobject = ρs N Vp

    Substitution: 2650 × 1 × 3.35103216e-17

    Result: 8.88023523e-14 kg

  10. Physical object-count reference

    Nobjects = (m₀ ×10⁻⁶)/mobject

    Substitution: (50.000000 ×10⁻⁶)/8.88023523e-14

    Result: 563048147.730762 objects; continuum average, not displayed marker count

  11. Original solid volume

    Vs = (m₀ ×10⁻⁶/ρs) ×10⁶

    Substitution: (50.000000 ×10⁻⁶/2650) ×10⁶

    Result: 0.01886792453 mL

  12. Initial mixture volume

    V₀ = Vwater+Vs

    Substitution: 100.000000+0.01886792453

    Result: 100.018868 mL

  13. Initial solid volume fraction

    φ₀ = Vs/V₀

    Substitution: 0.01886792453/100.018868

    Result: 1.88643652e-4 fraction

  14. Dilute object-envelope fraction

    φobjects = φ₀/φf

    Substitution: 1.88643652e-4/1.000000

    Result: 1.88643652e-4 fraction; below0.005 in allowed preparations

  15. Fluid-relative terminal reference

    vrel = 2(ρf−ρw)gRf²/(9µ)

    Substitution: 2 × (2650.000000−1000) × 9.81 × 2.00000000e-6²/(9 × 0.001)

    Result: 1.43880000e-5 m/s; equivalent-sphere low-Re reference

  16. Closed-batch front speed

    v = (1−φobjects)vrel

    Substitution: (1−1.88643652e-4) × 1.43880000e-5

    Result: 1.43852858e-5 m/s; dilute zero-volume-flux correction

  17. External carrier counterflow reference

    uw = φobjects vrel

    Substitution: 1.88643652e-4 × 1.43880000e-5

    Result: 2.71420487e-9 m/s upward during the uniform settling zone

  18. Particle Reynolds number

    Re = 2Rf ρw vrel/µ

    Substitution: 2 × 2.00000000e-6 × 1000 × 1.43880000e-5/0.001

    Result: 5.75520000e-5 dimensionless; below0.04 in allowed preparations

  19. Initial column height

    H = V₀/A

    Substitution: 100.018868/10

    Result: 10.001887 cm

  20. Wet-bed solid fraction

    β = packing ×φf

    Substitution: 0.6 × 1.000000

    Result: 0.6 fraction; chosen constant wet-bed packing

  21. Final wet-bed height

    Bmax = Vs/(Aβ)

    Substitution: 0.01886792453/(10 × 0.6)

    Result: 0.003144654088 cm

  22. Two-front settling completion

    tend = (H−Bmax)/(100v)

    Substitution: (10.001887−0.003144654088)/(100 × 1.43852858e-5)

    Result: 6950.673265 s; terminal-reference batch model

  23. Settled dry-mass fraction

    f = min(1,t/tend)

    Substitution: min(1,600.000000/6950.673265)

    Result: 0.08632257296 fraction

  24. Growing wet-bed height

    b = Bmax f

    Substitution: 0.003144654088 × 0.08632257296

    Result: 2.71454632e-4 cm

  25. Suspension upper front

    zcloud = max(b,H−100vt)

    Substitution: max(2.71454632e-4,10.001887−100 × 1.43852858e-5 × 600.000000)

    Result: 9.138770 cm above inside base

  26. Remaining suspension height

    hcloud = zcloud−b

    Substitution: 9.138770−2.71454632e-4

    Result: 9.138498 cm

  27. Clear upper-water depth

    hclear = H−zcloud

    Substitution: 10.001887−9.138770

    Result: 0.8631171477 cm

  28. Settled dry solid

    mbed = m₀f

    Substitution: 50.000000 × 0.08632257296

    Result: 4.316129 mg

  29. Suspended dry solid

    mcloud = m₀−mbed

    Substitution: 50.000000−4.316129

    Result: 45.683871 mg

  30. Settled solid-only volume

    Vs,bed = Vs f

    Substitution: 0.01886792453 × 0.08632257296

    Result: 0.001628727792 mL

  31. Suspended solid-only volume

    Vs,cloud = Vs−Vs,bed

    Substitution: 0.01886792453−0.001628727792

    Result: 0.01723919674 mL

  32. Wet-bed envelope volume

    Vbed = Ab

    Substitution: 10 × 2.71454632e-4

    Result: 0.002714546319 mL; mineral plus pore water

  33. Cloud envelope volume

    Vcloud = A hcloud

    Substitution: 10 × 9.138498

    Result: 91.384982 mL; mineral plus water

  34. Clear water volume

    Vclear = A hclear

    Substitution: 10 × 0.8631171477

    Result: 8.631171 mL

  35. Bed pore-water volume

    Vw,bed = Vbed−Vs,bed

    Substitution: 0.002714546319−0.001628727792

    Result: 0.001085818528 mL

  36. Cloud water volume

    Vw,cloud = Vcloud−Vs,cloud

    Substitution: 91.384982−0.01723919674

    Result: 91.367743 mL

  37. Retained water inventory

    Vw = Vw,bed+Vw,cloud+Vclear

    Substitution: 0.001085818528+91.367743+8.631171

    Result: 100.000000 mL; equals original water

  38. Local cloud concentration

    ccloud = mcloud/(Vcloud/1000)

    Substitution: 45.683871/(91.384982/1000)

    Result: 499.905678 mg/L; local cloud stays uniform until it disappears

  39. Effective gravity minus buoyancy

    Feff = (ρf−ρw)Vf g

    Substitution: (2650.000000−1000) × 3.35103216e-17 × 9.81

    Result: 5.42414821e-13 N

  40. Viscous terminal drag

    Fdrag = 6πµRf vrel

    Substitution: 6π × 0.001 × 2.00000000e-6 × 1.43880000e-5

    Result: 5.42414821e-13 N; equals effective gravity

  41. Wet-bed mass

    Mbed = ρw Vw,bed/1000+mbed/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 0.001085818528/1000+4.316129/1000

    Result: 0.005401947176 g; mL to water mass, mg to g

  42. Cloud mass

    Mcloud = ρw Vw,cloud/1000+mcloud/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 91.367743/1000+45.683871/1000

    Result: 91.413427 g

  43. Clear-water mass

    Mclear = ρw Vclear/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 8.631171/1000

    Result: 8.631171 g

  44. Initial whole-sample mass

    M₀ = ρw Vwater/1000+m₀/1000

    Substitution: 1000 × 100.000000/1000+50.000000/1000

    Result: 100.050000 g

  45. Whole-sample mass check

    M = Mbed+Mcloud+Mclear

    Substitution: 0.005401947176+91.413427+8.631171

    Result: 100.050000 g; equals initial mass

  46. Dry solid inventory check

    m = mbed+mcloud

    Substitution: 4.316129+45.683871

    Result: 50.000000 mg; equals original dry solid

  47. Terminal relaxation reference

    τ = 2ρf Rf²/(9µ)

    Substitution: 2 × 2650.000000 × 2.00000000e-6²/(9 × 0.001)

    Result: 2.35555556e-6 s; startup inertia omitted

Result, conclusion and limitations

Both jars conserve original water and dry solid. The porous wet bed is not dry powder, and clear appearance is not a purity measurement.

Prepared monodisperse ideal aggregate references, chosen effective drag envelopes, Df2, constant water properties, low-Re equivalent impermeable sphere drag, dilute volume counterflow, sharp batch fronts and constant saturated-bed packing. No coagulant/binder mass, dosing, measured aggregation/breakup kinetics, calibrated permeability, empirical hindering, Brownian transport, turbulence, ongoing bed compression, wall correction, startup inertia, filtration or dissolved-contaminant removal solver. Physical time is accelerated in the guide and explicitly labelled. Clear appearance does not establish purity or complete water treatment. Editorial class placement; quantitative settling formulas are teacher extensions.
